# 读方法响应形状探查

本报告只陈述观察。它不判断这些差异应当如何处理。

It does not promise the same response shape after a protocol or client change. A reproduction mismatch is a difference pending review, not an invalidation of this sample. web3_clientVersion is context only and is not a client attribution.
